 Hello Weihai EU,
 Some of your listings are at risk of deactivation due to missing mandatory compliance requirements for your products. Review the details and next steps below. The affected listings are shown at the end of this email.
 Why is this happening?
 We are taking this action because these products are missing mandatory compliance information. Review our Product safety and compliance help page for information on applicable laws, regulations, and Amazon policies.
 The table at the end of this email provides product-specific compliance requirements.
 How do I reactivate my listings?
 To provide the required compliance information, go to your Account Health page and locate the product compliance requests:
-
 If you have the required documentation, follow the instructions to submit your compliance documents.
-
 If you believe your products are exempt from these requirements, submit documentation demonstrating why they are not subject to these compliance requirements.
 For step-by-step instructions, see our Submit compliance documents or appeal a documentation request help page.
 To avoid further impact to your account, close or delete any non-compliant listings that you do

RE:[CASE 20858098381] US - Food and Product Safety - ASIN: B0DD7R3PPG
Hello from Amazon Selling Partner Support,
We are writing regarding your dispute for ASIN B0DD7R3PPG (SKU: aadw) — Children Painting Set, which was suppressed due to the non-submission of required details to the Testing Inspection Certification (TIC) team for a non-compliant product with a "New" TRF status.
We can confirm that your appeal submission is currently under review. According to your Account Health Dashboard, we have received your submission and the evaluation is in progress — no further action is required from you at this time. Please continue to monitor the status of your appeal directly from your Account Health Dashboard:
https://sellercentral.amazon.com/performance/account/health/product-policies?s=aadw
In the meantime, for guidance on reinstatement requirements, please review the details under Performance Notifications in your Account Health Dashboard :
https://sellercentral.amazon.com/help/hub/reference/GKJRA3FRPRKVBJJS?locale=en-US
To help us continually improve, we ask that you take a moment to complete our survey below to tell us about your experience with this specific interaction.

Your VAT invoice for EPR Pay on Behalf charges for Amazon.co.uk sales
 96
 Hello,
Your invoice for Amazon.co.uk Extended Producer Responsibility (EPR) Pay on Behalf charges is now available.
The invoice includes (1) the eco-contribution fees that Amazon paid on your behalf for sales of products subject to EPR obligations on Amazon.co.uk during the applicable reporting period, and (2) EPR Pay on Behalf service fees for the EPR categories specified in the category-level and ASIN-level summary.
To access your EPR Pay on Behalf invoice , open the attachment in this email or go to Seller Fee Invoices and search for "EPR Pay on Behalf" in the 'Invoice Type' column.
For a category-level summary of the fees charged, applicable reporting periods, and EPR categories, go to Transaction View with Service Fees as Transaction type, and search for "EPR" in the "Product Details" column.
For an ASIN-level summary of your EPR eco-contribution fees, applicable reporting periods, and EPR categories and sub-categories, go to EPR Category Reports .

FBA reimbursement notification
 You have a reimbursement activity
 Hello from Fulfilment by Amazon,
We have initiated a reimbursement, a reimbursement reversal, or both due to recent activity on your selling account. No action is required from you.
Reimbursements can be initiated for an issue that you notified us about or one that we identified at a fulfilment centre. A reimbursement reversal occurs when a reimbursement decision has been reviewed and changed. Reimbursement amounts are determined in accordance with the FBA inventory reimbursement policy ( https://sellercentral.amazon.co.uk/gp/help/external/200213130 ).
Please note that it may take up to five business days for reimbursement transactions to appear in your seller account.
You can find details on your reimbursement or reversal transaction in the Reimbursements report ( https://sellercentral.amazon.co.uk/reportcentral/REIMBURSEMENTS/0 ) in Seller Central:
- In the Event Date drop-down menu, select a date range and click Generate report . You can use the date in the subject line of this email as a point of reference.
- To identify inventory reimbursements and reversals, see the Quantity reimbursed [inventory] column.

 You can now download your quarterly tax report, which includes data such as identity information, number of transactions, revenue, and commission and service fees.
 Hello,
 According to rules for online stores issued by the State Council of China (Decree No. 810), we are required to provide information on China-based sellers to the Chinese tax authority on a quarterly basis.
 Some sellers identified potential data discrepancies with the first quarterly tax report covering January to March 2026. Our team has carefully reviewed all reporting to ensure it accurately reflects your activity in our store and resubmitted Q1 2026 reports to the Chinese tax authority where necessary.
 We’re re-sending your quarterly China tax report in case you didn’t download it. The links below are active for seven days.
 Download your quarterly report
 This report is provided for your reference only; no action is required.

Our team has carefully reviewed all reporting to ensure it accurately reflects your activity in our store, and resubmitted Q1 2026 reports to the Chinese tax authority where necessary.
 We’re re-sending your quarterly China tax report in case you didn’t download it. The links below are active for seven days.
 Download your quarterly report
 This report is provided for your reference only, no action is required.
 We’ve also provided a more granular order-level report that displays your quarterly information by transaction.
 Download your order-level report
 Variation between the data in these reports and your payment and Amazon VAT transactions report is expected. Each report uses distinct

时间分组：last7 邮箱：account_4 发件人：Amazon Services <donotreply@amazon.com> 日期：Wed, 1 Jul 2026 16:04:35 +0000 命中词： 抓取方式：RETR 检测时间：2026-07-06 22:59:34
展开查看邮件预览 
96
 La normativa portuguesa sobre la RAP se aplica a los productos vendidos a clientes de Portugal.
 Hola,
Para cumplir la legislación portuguesa sobre la responsabilidad ampliada del productor (RAP) , tienes que obtener números de registro de responsabilidad ampliada del productor para los productos que vendas a clientes de Portugal.
La RAP es un principio de política medioambiental que hace que el productor sea responsable de los productos a lo largo de todo su ciclo de vida. Esto incluye todo, desde el diseño del producto hasta la gestión al final de su vida útil, como la recogida, clasificación y reciclaje de residuos.
Por lo general, se entiende por "productor" a la parte que comercializa por primera vez en un país un producto sujeto a la normativa RAP.
Tienes que presentarnos los números de registro de la RAP para las siguientes categorías de productos reguladas:
- Embalaje
- Pilas y baterías Para presentarnos tu número de registro de la normativa y demostrar su cumplimiento normativo, sigue los pasos que se indican a continuación:
- En tu cuenta de Seller Central de España, ve a  Estado de la cuenta .
- Selecciona  Cumplimiento normativo .
- Selecciona  Enviar información so

FBA reimbursement notification
 You have a reimbursement activity
 Hello from Fulfilment by Amazon,
We have initiated a reimbursement, a reimbursement reversal, or both due to recent activity on your selling account. No action is required from you.
Reimbursements can be initiated for an issue that you notified us about or one that we identified at a fulfilment centre. A reimbursement reversal occurs when a reimbursement decision has been reviewed and changed. Reimbursement amounts are determined in accordance with the FBA inventory reimbursement policy ( https://sellercentral.amazon.co.uk/gp/help/external/200213130 ).
Please note that it may take up to five business days for reimbursement transactions to appear in your seller account.
You can find details on your reimbursement or reversal transaction in the Reimbursements report ( https://sellercentral.amazon.co.uk/reportcentral/REIMBURSEMENTS/0 ) in Seller Central:
- In the Event Date drop-down menu, select a date range and click Generate report . You can use the date in the subject line of this email as a point of reference.
- To identify inventory reimbursements and reversals, see the Quantity reimbursed [inventory] column.

FBA reimbursement notification
 You have a reimbursement activity
 Hello from Fulfilment by Amazon,
We have initiated a reimbursement, a reimbursement reversal, or both due to recent activity on your selling account. No action is required from you.
Reimbursements can be initiated for an issue that you notified us about or one that we identified at a fulfilment centre. A reimbursement reversal occurs when a reimbursement decision has been reviewed and changed. Reimbursement amounts are determined in accordance with the FBA inventory reimbursement policy ( https://sellercentral.amazon.co.uk/gp/help/external/200213130 ).
Please note that it may take up to five business days for reimbursement transactions to appear in your seller account.
You can find details on your reimbursement or reversal transaction in the Reimbursements report ( https://sellercentral.amazon.co.uk/reportcentral/REIMBURSEMENTS/0 ) in Seller Central:
- In the Event Date drop-down menu, select a date range and click Generate report . You can use the date in the subject line of this email as a point of reference.
- To identify inventory reimbursements and reversals, see the Quantity reimbursed [inventory] column.

FBA reimbursement notification
时间分组：older 邮箱：account_4 发件人："Fulfillment by Amazon (Do not reply)" <donotreply@amazon.com> 日期：Fri, 12 Jun 2026 03:06:46 +0000 命中词： 抓取方式：RETR 检测时间：2026-07-06 22:58:20
展开查看邮件预览 
96
FBA reimbursement notification
 You have a reimbursement activity
 Hello from Fulfilment by Amazon,
We have initiated a reimbursement, a reimbursement reversal, or both due to recent activity on your selling account. No action is required from you.
Reimbursements can be initiated for an issue that you notified us about or one that we identified at a fulfilment centre. A reimbursement reversal occurs when a reimbursement decision has been reviewed and changed. Reimbursement amounts are determined in accordance with the FBA inventory reimbursement policy ( https://sellercentral.amazon.co.uk/gp/help/external/200213130 ).
Please note that it may take up to five business days for reimbursement transactions to appear in your seller account.
You can find details on your reimbursement or reversal transaction in the Reimbursements report ( https://sellercentral.amazon.co.uk/reportcentral/REIMBURSEMENTS/0 ) in Seller Central:
- In the Event Date drop-down menu, select a date range and click Generate report . You can use the date in the subject line of this email as a point of reference.
- To identify inventory reimbursements and reversals, see the Quantity reimbursed [inventory] column.

 Hello,
 According to rules for online stores issued by the State Council of China (Decree No. 810), we are required to provide information on China-based sellers to the Chinese tax authority on a quarterly basis.
 Some sellers identified potential data discrepancies with the first quarterly tax report covering January to March 2026. Our team has carefully reviewed all reporting to ensure it accurately reflects your activity in our store and resubmitted Q1 2026 reports to the Chinese tax authority where necessary.

 Did your sales cover all fees and returns for this statement period? The payment amount reflects sales activities, selling fees and fees for promotions or services. We recommend reviewing your Payment Report under the Reports tab in Seller Central to understand your closing balance.
To learn more about Payment Reports in Seller Central, please visit our official course.
We wish you continued success!

重要信息：即刻重新验证您的所有 付款账户或存款方式
时间分组：older 邮箱：account_4 发件人：Seller Central <no-reply@amazon.com> 日期：Tue, 16 Jun 2026 06:40:46 +0000 命中词： 抓取方式：RETR 检测时间：2026-07-06 22:58:34
展开查看邮件预览 
96
 亲爱的卖家，
 Amazon Payments UK (APUK) Limited 为持有牌照的支付服务提供商，为开设和维护“我要开店”账户，受特定的义务约束。
 从现在开始，您将无法使用未经验证的银行账户收款。 但是，您仍可以使用您卖家账户关联的已验证的银行账户收款，并继续在亚马逊上发布和销售。
如果您已验证您的卖家账户关联的所有银行账户，您无需采取任何行动。
 如果您有任何收款账户未经验证，为了继续接收付款，请按照以下步骤进行操作：
 1. 前往卖家平台的“银行账户信息” (列在支付信息)页面找到您的银行账户信息。https://sellercentral-europe.amazon.com/sw/AccountInfo/DepositMethodView/step/DepositMethodView
 2. 单击每个银行账户对应的“更换存款方式”。
 3. 输入您的银行账户信息。如果您注册的是企业卖家，所输入的银行账户必须是企业账户。
 4. 单击“设置存款方式”。
 5. 上传新账户的银行对账单或银行资信证明的扫描件。
 6. 上传文件后，检查您的公司信息部分，然后单击“提交”按钮以完成提交信息。
 文件必须满足以下标准：
 1. 文件必须是在最近180 天内开具的。
 2. 文件必须显示银行账号、银行徽标和账户持有者姓名。
 3. 如果您以企业形式开展业务，则您的银行账户必须为企业账户。
 4. 请务必确保文件清晰易读。
 5. 文件不能是屏幕截图。
文件必须使用我们支持的语言。我们支持的语言为英语、中文、荷兰语、德语、意大利语、西班牙语、瑞典语、法语、土耳其语、波兰语、葡萄牙语、阿拉伯语、印地语、泰米尔语、越南语、泰语和日语。如果原始文件不是我们支持的语言，请提供原始文件和认证翻译的副本。
 我们愿意随时为您提供帮助。如果您对此验证过程存有疑问，请联系我们的卖家支持团队。
 我们收集任何关于“我要开店” 的信息均依据亚马逊支付隐私声明。
 顺颂商祺亚马逊支付团队
 Amazon Payments UK Limited 是一家在英格兰和威尔士注册的私人股份有限公司（注册编码：11049457；增值税税号： GB295 7604 60），注册地址为 1 Principal Place, Worship St, London, EC2A 2FA, United Kingdom。Amazon Payments UK Limited 经英国金融行为监管局根据《2017 年支付服务条例》（参考编号：799814）授权，可提供支付服务。

 245,82.

New EU restriction on textile and footwear disposal
时间分组：older 邮箱：account_4 发件人：Amazon Europe <donotreply@amazon.com> 日期：Wed, 10 Jun 2026 19:04:36 +0000 命中词： 抓取方式：RETR 检测时间：2026-07-06 22:58:09
展开查看邮件预览 
96
 By July 19, update your removal settings for apparel, clothing and footwear in EU fulfillment centers
 Hello,
Starting July 19, 2026 , new EU requirements under the Ecodesign for Sustainable Products Regulation (ESPR) will restrict the destruction of certain unsold products, including apparel, clothing accessories, and footwear.
 With this change, you may incur a fine for the disposal of these products in line with local country requirements, based on your business size and disposal volume.
If you sell textiles or footwear in the EU, we recommend that you update your FBA removal settings to Return, Liquidate, or Donate (where eligible) before July 19, 2026.
To review removal options and eligibility, go to Remove Inventory from a Fulfillment Center .
 Note: Products that aren’t eligible for donation or liquidation may require disposal in line with relevant regulations and safety guidelines. This may impact your ESPR compliance status.
Also, on June 25, we’ll update the Fulfillment by Amazon (FBA) Donations terms and conditions in accordance with the above new requirement, our increased cross-border donation capabilities for inventory in Europe, and for program consistency.
To le

Your VAT invoice for EPR Pay on Behalf charges for Amazon.co.uk sales
 96
 Hello,
Your invoice for Amazon.co.uk Extended Producer Responsibility (EPR) Pay on Behalf charges is now available.
The invoice includes (1) the eco-contribution fees that Amazon paid on your behalf for sales of products subject to EPR obligations on Amazon.co.uk during the applicable reporting period, and (2) EPR Pay on Behalf service fees for the EPR categories specified in the category-level and ASIN-level summary.
To access your EPR Pay on Behalf invoice , open the attachment in this email or go to Seller Fee Invoices and search for "EPR Pay on Behalf" in the 'Invoice Type' column.
For a category-level summary of the fees charged, applicable reporting periods, and EPR categories, go to Transaction View with Service Fees as Transaction type, and search for "EPR" in the "Product Details" column.
For an ASIN-level summary of your EPR eco-contribution fees, applicable reporting periods, and EPR categories and sub-categories, go to EPR Category Reports .

Presentar información de cumplimiento normativo de la RAP en Portugal
时间分组：last7 邮箱：account_4 发件人：Amazon Services <donotreply@amazon.com> 日期：Wed, 1 Jul 2026 16:04:35 +0000 命中词： 抓取方式：RETR 检测时间：2026-07-06 22:59:34
展开查看邮件预览 
96
 La normativa portuguesa sobre la RAP se aplica a los productos vendidos a clientes de Portugal.
 Hola,
Para cumplir la legislación portuguesa sobre la responsabilidad ampliada del productor (RAP) , tienes que obtener números de registro de responsabilidad ampliada del productor para los productos que vendas a clientes de Portugal.
La RAP es un principio de política medioambiental que hace que el productor sea responsable de los productos a lo largo de todo su ciclo de vida. Esto incluye todo, desde el diseño del producto hasta la gestión al final de su vida útil, como la recogida, clasificación y reciclaje de residuos.
Por lo general, se entiende por "productor" a la parte que comercializa por primera vez en un país un producto sujeto a la normativa RAP.
Tienes que presentarnos los números de registro de la RAP para las siguientes categorías de productos reguladas:
- Embalaje
- Pilas y baterías Para presentarnos tu número de registro de la normativa y demostrar su cumplimiento normativo, sigue los pasos que se indican a continuación:
- En tu cuenta de Seller Central de España, ve a  Estado de la cuenta .
- Selecciona  Cumplimiento normativo .
- Selecciona  Enviar información so

 Ponownie przesyłamy kwartalny raport podatkowy, który obejmuje dane takie jak informacje o tożsamości, liczba transakcji, przychody oraz prowizje i opłaty za usługi.
 Dzień dobry,
 23 czerwca 2026 r. po weryfikacji ewentualnych rozbieżności w danych wysłaliśmy zaktualizowany kwartalny raport podatkowy dotyczący Chin za okres od października do grudnia 2025 r.
 Jeśli pobranie raportu nie było możliwe, przesyłamy go ponownie. Poniższe łącza są aktywne przez pięć dni.
 Pobierz kwartalny raport
 Raport udostępniamy wyłącznie w celach informacyjnych. Nie są wymagane żadne działania.
 Udostępniliśmy również bardziej szczegółowy raport na poziomie zamówień, który przedstawia informacje kwartalne z podziałem na transakcje.
 Pobierz raport na poziomie zamówień
 Mogą wystąpić rozbieżności między danymi w tych raportach a raportem o płatnościach oraz raportem Amazon o transakcjach podlegających VAT. Każdy raport wykorzystuje odrębną metodologię i służy do innego celu.
 Będziemy nadal przekazywać kwartalne aktualizacje dotyczące danych udostępnianych chińskim organom podatkowym.
 Więcej informacji znajdziesz na stronie Wymogi dotyczące zgłaszania informacji podatkowych w Chinach .

 Tentativo di pagamento

In data 06/21/26 12:49 CEST, abbiamo avviato un trasferimento sul tuo metodo di pagamento (conto bancario che termina con 229) dell’importo di €
 204,78.
 Se l’importo è inferiore a quanto atteso, tieni in considerazione le seguenti domande:
 Hai un saldo non disponibile? Amazon potrebbe trattenere una certa somma per coprire i costi di eventuali resi, reclami o contestazioni di una transazione (chargeback) da parte degli acquirenti. In questo caso, il saldo finale nel tuo report sui pagamenti mostrerà il saldo non disponibile corrispondente alla cifra trattenuta.
 Le tue vendite coprono tutte le commissioni e i resi per questo ciclo di fatturazione? L’importo del pagamento rispecchia le attività di vendita, le commissioni di vendita e le tariffe per promozioni o servizi. 时间分组：older 邮箱：account_4 发件人：Amazon Services <donotreply@amazon.com> 日期：Wed, 17 Jun 2026 17:13:19 +0000 命中词： 抓取方式：RETR 检测时间：2026-07-06 22:58:45

 Effective July 30, 2026, the New Selection Program (2026) launches with increased benefits to help you launch new branded products with reduced costs and complexity while boosting early sales.
 Hello,
 If you’re currently enrolled in our existing New Selection Program , you’ll automatically receive New Selection Program (2026) benefits for new branded FBA ASINs that launch starting July 30, through October 31, 2026, as an introductory offer.
 To continue to receive New Selection Program (2026) benefits after October 31, you must confirm your enrollment on Enroll in New Selection Program (2026) . From July 30, the existing New Selection Program will end.

 您的部分商品因缺少必要的商品合规信息而面临被停售的风险。查看下面的详细信息和后续步骤。受影响的商品显示在此电子邮件的末尾。
 为什么会发生这种情况？
 我们之所以采取此措施，是因为这些商品缺少必要的合规信息。请查看我们的 商品安全与合规 帮助页面，了解有关适用法律法规和亚马逊政策的信息。
 此电子邮件末尾的表格提供了特定于商品的合规性要求。
 如何重新启售商品？

-

-
 如果您认为您的商品无需遵守这些要求，请提交相关文件，说明它们为何不受这些合规要求的约束。
 有关分步操作说明，请参阅 提交合规文件或对文件请求提出申诉 帮助页面。
 为避免进一步影响您的账户，请停售或删除您不打算销售或申诉的任何不合规商品。
 如果我未执行上述操作，会怎么样？
 如果您未在“账户状况”页面显示的截止日期之前提供所需的合规信息，我们可能会停售受影响的商品。
 我们愿意随时为您提供帮助。
 有关更多信息，请观看卖家大学中的 安全与合规简介 视频
 如果您有其他问题，请在您的 账户状况 控制面板上提交请求。

 Para ayudarte a mantener el distintivo de Prime durante Prime Day, hemos actualizado nuestros requisitos del distintivo en España.
 Hola:
 Durante Prime Day, actualizaremos temporalmente nuestros requisitos del distintivo en España para ayudarte a mantener el distintivo de Prime.
 Del 14 de junio al 4 de julio de 2026, el 85 % de las ofertas aptas para Prime con las velocidades de entrega más rápidas recibirán el distintivo de Prime, siempre que tengan una velocidad de entrega de 7 días o menos . Este cambio se aplica a todas las ofertas de Logística de Amazon, gestionadas por el vendedor y de venta minorista de Amazon.
 Los requisitos habituales del distintivo de Prime volverán a aplicarse el 5 de julio de 2026.
 Agradecemos tu esfuerzo por ofrecer un gran servicio de compra a los clientes Prime durante este Prime Day.

 Actualización de estado de mercancías peligrosas
 Estimado vendedor:
 Amazon lleva a cabo regularmente revisiones de productos que pueden estar regulados como mercancía peligrosa (también conocidos como material peligroso) en sus centros logísticos. Como resultado de una de estas revisiones, el estado de mercancías peligrosas de uno o más de tus productos ha cambiado. Przeprowadzamy te przeglądy, aby zapewnić zgodność naszych przesyłek z wymogami regulacyjnymi i normami bezpieczeństwa dla naszych klientów i pracowników.  Update van status gevaarlijke goederen
 Hallo van het Fulfillment by Amazon-team,
 Amazon voert regelmatig controles uit bij producten in onze fulfillment centers die onder regelgeving van gevaarlijke goederen zouden kunnen vallen (ook bekend als hazmat of gevaarlijke stoffen). We voeren deze controles uit om ervoor te zorgen dat onze zendingen voldoen aan de wettelijke vereisten en veiligheidsnormen voor onze klanten en werknemers. Na een dergelijke controle is de status van gevaarlijke goederen van een of meer van je producten veranderd. Lees onderstaande informatie voor meer informatie over wat dit betekent voor je bestaande voorraad en nieuwe zendingen.
 LEVERBARE ITEMS
 De volgende producten waren voorheen niet leverbaar via Fulfillment by Amazon of waren niet langer toegestaan in zendingen naar Amazon Fulfillment Centers. Deze beperkingen zijn opgeheven. Onderstaande producten kunnen door Amazon worden geleverd of aan jou worden geretourneerd en je kunt weer nieuwe eenheden naar fulfillment centers versturen.

 Tytuły we wszystkich kategoriach (z wyjątkiem kategorii „Media”) będą musiały zawierać 75 znaków lub mniej, łącznie ze spacjami. Nowa funkcja „Najważniejsze informacje” zapewnia dodatkowe 125 znaków, umożliwiając udostępnianie materiałów.

 Począwszy od 27 lipca 2026 r. tytuły we wszystkich kategoriach (z wyjątkiem kategorii „Media”) będą musiały zawierać 75 znaków lub mniej, łącznie ze spacjami. 时间分组：older 邮箱：account_4 发件人：Amazon Europe <donotreply@amazon.com> 日期：Wed, 10 Jun 2026 19:03:48 +0000 命中词： 抓取方式：RETR 检测时间：2026-07-06 22:58:08
展开查看邮件预览 
96
 Aggiorna le impostazioni di rimozione per capi di abbigliamento, accessori e calzature nei centri logistici dell'Unione europea entro il 19 luglio
 Buongiorno,
a partire dal 19 luglio 2026 , i nuovi requisiti dell'Unione europea previsti dalle normative di progettazione ecocompatibile per prodotti sostenibili (ESPR) stabiliranno dei limiti alla distruzione di alcuni prodotti invenduti, tra cui abbigliamento, accessori e calzature.
 Di conseguenza, la distruzione di tali prodotti potrebbe esporti al rischio di multe, in base ai requisiti locali, alle dimensioni della tua attività e al volume di articoli distrutti.
Se vendi prodotti tessili o calzature nell'Unione europea, ti consigliamo di aggiornare le impostazioni di rimozione di Logistica di Amazon su Restituzione, Liquidazione o Donazione (ove disponibili) entro il 19 luglio 2026.
Per verificare le opzioni di rimozione e l'idoneità, consulta Rimuovere l'inventario da un centro logistico .
 Nota: I prodotti non idonei alla donazione o alla liquidazione potrebbero richiedere il reimpiego da parte di Amazon in conformità alle normative pertinenti e alle linee guida sulla sicurezza. Ciò potrebbe influire sullo stato di conformit

 Do 19 lipca zmień ustawienia wycofywania zapasów odzieży i obuwia magazynowanych w centrach logistycznych w UE
 Dzień dobry,
od 19 lipca 2026 r. możliwość niszczenia niektórych niesprzedanych produktów, w tym odzieży, akcesoriów odzieżowych i obuwia, zostanie ograniczona. Wynika to z nowych wymogów UE ustanowionych w rozporządzeniu w sprawie ekoprojektu dla zrównoważonych produktów (ESPR) .
 W związku z tą zmianą możesz otrzymać karę za usunięcie takich produktów zgodnie z wymogami danego kraju. Będzie ona zależeć od wielkości Twojego biznesu i usuwanych ilości.
Jeśli prowadzisz sprzedaż tekstyliów lub obuwia w UE, przed 19 lipca 2026 r. zmień ustawienia wycofywania zapasów FBA na „Zwrot”, „Likwidacja” lub „Darowizna” (o ile opcja ta jest dostępna).
Aby poznać opcje wycofywania i warunki kwalifikacji, przejdź na stronę Wycofywanie zapasów z centrum logistycznego .
 Uwaga: Produkty, które nie kwalifikują się do likwidacji ani do przekazania jako darowizna, mogą wymagać usunięcia zgodnie z odpowiednimi przepisami i wytycznymi dotyczącymi bezpieczeństwa. Może to wpłynąć na Twoją zgodność z rozporządzeniem ESPR.

Nouvelle restriction de l’UE sur la mise au rebut des textiles et des chaussures
时间分组：older 邮箱：account_4 发件人：Amazon Europe <donotreply@amazon.com> 日期：Wed, 10 Jun 2026 18:08:34 +0000 命中词： 抓取方式：RETR 检测时间：2026-07-06 22:58:07
展开查看邮件预览 
96
 D’ici le 19 juillet, mettez à jour vos paramètres de demande de prélèvement pour les vêtements, les accessoires vestimentaires et les chaussures dans les centres de distribution de l’UE
 Bonjour,
À partir du 19 juillet 2026 , de nouvelles exigences de l’UE prévues par le Règlement sur l’écoconception des produits durables (ESPR) limiteront la destruction de certains produits invendus, notamment les vêtements, les accessoires vestimentaires et les chaussures.
 Avec ce changement, vous risquez une amende pour la mise au rebut de ces produits conformément aux exigences locales, selon la taille de votre entreprise et le volume de mise au rebut.
Si vous vendez des textiles ou des chaussures dans l’UE, nous vous recommandons de mettre à jour vos paramètres de prélèvement Expédié par Amazon sur Retour, Liquider ou Faire un don (si applicable) avant le 19 juillet 2026.
Pour consulter les options de prélèvement et l’éligibilité, accédez à Prélever le stock d’un centre de distribution .

[Amazon.co.uk] One or More of Your Amazon Listings Have Been Search Suppressed
 [Amazon.co.uk] One or More of Your Amazon Listings Have Been Search Suppressed
 One or more of your Amazon UK listings have been suppressed from search results
 Amazon took action on one or more of your listings. 1 listing(s) were quarantined and removed from search results on the Amazon website. The affected listings do not meet Amazon’s ASIN Creation Policy , Duplicate ASIN Creation Policy , Product Image Requirements or Product Title Requirements .
 Review and edit quarantined listings Affected Listings
 ASIN SKU Enforcement Reason B0H11DGDRL WH-DE-0508-HCGD-02 More Information Required
 How do I resolve quarantined listings?
 Review and edit the listing(s) on the Suppressed Inventory page in Seller Central to correct the affected attributes or images.
 Once the affected attributes or images have been corrected, the listing(s) will be removed from quarantine and made available in search results.

 Hello from Amazon Seller Services,
 The products listed below have been enrolled in the Transparency programme by the brand owner. This means that the product is manufactured with a serial number. The serial number can be either a 2D Transparency branded barcode (these are 26 characters long and include a ‘T’ logo) or a 1D or 2D serial number barcode (these are between 7 and 20 characters long and may include the prefix ‘SN’).
 To ensure product authenticity for your customers, we will require that these products have a Transparency barcode or serial number barcode affixed to the outer packaging of each individual unit of the product in order to be accepted by Amazon.
 Below is the list of ASINs that will require a Transparency barcode or serial number barcode from the manufacturer or brand.
 ASIN
 Title
 B0H11DGDRL Roaxkois
 Amazon scans these barcode labels to verify the authenticity of the products. Your customers can also scan your Transparency barcode with the Transparency mobile app to confirm the item is not a counterfeit.
 Please contact the respective Brand or your distributor for any further information on the availability of the products with these barcodes.

时间分组：older 邮箱：account_4 发件人：Amazon Services <donotreply@amazon.com> 日期：Wed, 10 Jun 2026 20:33:42 +0000 命中词： 抓取方式：RETR 检测时间：2026-07-06 22:58:13

96
 Titles in all categories except for media will need to be 75 characters or less including spaces. A new Item Highlights feature now provides an additional 125 characters for sharing materials.
 Hello,
 Your product titles are one of the first things customers see, and for customers shopping on mobile, every character counts. To ensure your product titles appeal to customers everywhere, we’re making some changes that will help you show your product name and your product highlights seamlessly.
 Starting July 27, 2026 , titles in all categories except for media will need to be 75 characters or less including spaces. This ensures that your title will fully display in mobile and is consistent with the title length used by other online stores.
 Item Highlights provides an additional 125 characters for sharing materials or recommended use cases that help customers compare options. This content is searchable and visible below titles in search results and on product detail pages.
 We’re providing you with AI-powered tools to ensure that your new titles and Item Highlights are optimal. You can start reviewing, modifying, and using AI-recommended titles and Item Highlights today by taking

96
Fix stranded inventory
 Take action to prevent unnecessary removal of your stranded inventory
 Review stranded inventory scheduled for automatic removal
 You are receiving this message because you have stranded inventory in an Amazon fulfillment center that is scheduled for automatic removal. For a full view of stranded units scheduled for automatic removal, visit the stranded inventory dashboard and sort by Auto removal date.
 Review stranded inventory
 Why is this happening?
 Inventory can be scheduled for automatic removal for a variety of reasons. In this case, the reason is that the inventory is stranded. Stranded inventory is inventory without a buyable offer and requires you to either reactivate the listing or remove the inventory.
 What happens if I don't take action?
 Your stranded inventory will be returned or disposed of based on the settings you selected on "Fix stranded inventory". To customize your settings, go to Fix stranded inventory and select Edit automatic action settings.
 If you need more time to appeal or fix a stranded inventory issue, go to Fix stranded inventory and select Delay auto removal for 30 days from the drop-down menu for each listing.
 Report

时间分组：older 邮箱：account_4 发件人：transparency-queue <transparency-queue@amazon.com> 日期：Sun, 7 Jun 2026 07:30:07 +0000 命中词： 抓取方式：RETR 检测时间：2026-07-06 22:57:45

尊敬的Transparency(透明计划)客户：
您好！感谢您在Transparency(透明计划)中注册您的商品。
下表中列出的商品在运营表现评估(OPR)期间贴标表现较差，或在过去的90天内发货数量不足，或代码请求数量为零。我们需要了解您已采取的措施，以进行OPR评估，评估因素可能包括下一批货件的预计发送日期以及Transparency透明计划代码的使用状态，或为防止错误贴标而采取的措施等。
请在接下来的14天内联系您的Transparency(透明计划)经理或者此邮件抄送的客户管理团队，告知以上问题的具体原因和您接下来的行动计划。如果在接下来的14 天内，您并没有联系您的Transparency(透明计划)经理或者此邮件抄送的客户管理团队，或您的商品依然存在以下某项问题，我们将停止该商品的OPR评估： 1) 自该商品开启OPR后的90天内，FBA仓库未收到该商品的贴标货物。 2) 在过去90天收到的贴标货物的贴标准确率低于1%。 3) 自该商品开启OPR后的90天内，未申请任何Transparency透明计划代码或标签。
请注意，Transparency(透明计划)要求每一个独一无二的代码能被正确应用并扫描，以便买家和亚马逊能够可靠地验证每一件商品。如果我们收到未应用Transparency(透明计划)标签或错误应用标签的商品，或者未收到商品货件，这会影响我们对您的商品正品的验证，并影响您享受此项服务的权益。
**表1：在过去的90天内未收到用于OPR评估的商品：
GTIN    ASIN    Product title   # of units received     Total success % % of units with no codes        % of units with incorrect codes
00702291990335  B0FF2ZGWR6      Crifeton        0       0       0       0
00702291990328  B0FF354HSJ      Crifeton        0       0       0       0
*此电子邮件中的列表可能并未显示您的所有商品。我们仅列举出需要引起您注意的商品。
如果您发现您的商品已变成Onboarding状态，并且您希望重新打开OPR， 请您登录Transparency透明计划自助平台重新启用OPR。 请勿回复邮件至 transparency-queue@amazon.com。如果您有任何问题, 请联系您的Transparency(透明计划)经理或此邮件抄送的客户管理团队。

Transparency(透明计划)团队
